LP build A efficient:
```
def _build_q_cg(q, n):
    # returns A (2n-1, n) such that (A g)[l] = conv(q,g)[l]
    A = np.empty((2*n-1, n), dtype=float)
    qr = q[::-1]
    for l in range(2*n-1):
        start = max(0, l-n+1)
        end = min(n-1, l)
        A[l, start:end+1] = qr[l:end+1 -? ]
```

```
act = np.where(p > max(1e-5, p.max()*1e-4))[0]
if act.size ==0: act=[b.argmax()]
grad=np.zeros(n)
for l in act:
    w=2*p[l]
    s=max(0,l-n+1); e=min(n-1,l)
    grad[s:e+1] += w * q[l-e+1:l-s+1][::-1]
```

`_grad(q, mode, temp)`:

Helper `_score_mode(a, mode)`:
- Ensure a is np array.
- If a.size==0 return inf.
- s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf
- b = np.convolve(a,a)
- if mode=='off':
   if a.size>1:
      # b length 2n-1
      if b.size <=1: mb=b[0]
      else:
         # use slicing avoid copy: mb = max(b[:n-1].max(), b[n:].max())
         mb = max(float(np.max(b[:n-1])) if n-1>0 else -inf, float(np.max(b[n:])) if n>0 else -inf)
   else mb=b[0]
  else mb = float(np.max(b))
  val = 2.0*a.size*mb/(s*s)
  return val if finite else inf
```

Helper `_gradient(q, mode, tau)`:
```
def _gradient(q, mode, tau):
   n=q.size; L=2*n-1; b=np.convolve(q,q); z=n-1
   mask = np.ones(L,bool)
   if mode=='off' and n>1:
       mask = np.ones(L,bool); mask[z]=False
   # if no mask, full
   if tau <=0 or not finite: tau = 1e-8
   # compute bmax over mask
   if not mask.any():
       bmax = float(b.max())
   else:
       bmax = float(np.max(b[mask]))
   if not finite or bmax <=0: return np.zeros(n), bmax
   x = np.full(L, -np.inf, dtype=float)
   x[mask] = (b[mask] - bmax) / tau
   # max x =0
   p = np.exp(x)  # x masked -inf
   p_sum = p.sum()
   if p_sum <=0 or not finite:
       p = np.zeros(L); p[np.argmax(np.where(mask,b,-np.inf))]=1.0
   else:
       p /= p_sum
   # threshold
   pm = p.max()
   thr = max(1e-8, 1e-4*pm)
   act = np.where(p > thr)[0]
   if act.size ==0:
       act = np.array([int(np.argmax(p))])
   elif act.size > 64: # maybe 96
       ord = np.argsort(p[act])[-64:]
       act = act[ord]
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   for l in act:
       w=2.0*float(p[l])
       if w ==0: continue
       s0 = l - (n-1)
       if s0 <0: s0=0
       e0 = l
       if e0 >= n: e0 = n-1
       if s0 <= e0:
          grad[s0:e0+1] += w * q[l - e0 + 1 : l - s0 + 1][::-1]
   return grad, bmax
```
Check slices: `l-e0+1` and `l-s0+1`. Example l=0: s0=0,e0=0 -> q[1:1? l-e0+1=1, l-s0+1=1] empty? Let's recalc. q indices from l-e0 to l-s0. e0=0, s0=0: indices [0:1] -> q[0]. Slice `q[l-e0+1 : l-s0+1]` = q[1:1] empty. Oops off by one. Need slice from l-e0 to l-s0 inclusive. Python slice start inclusive, stop exclusive. If we want reversed, take forward slice `q[l-e0 : l-s0+1][::-1]`. For l=0: q[0:1][::-1]=q[0]. For l=3,n=5: s0=0,e0=3 -> indices 0:4 reversed q[3,2,1,0]. Good. `_normalize(a)`:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return a
   a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
   s = float(a.sum())
   if not finite or s <= 0:
       return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(1,a.size))
   a = a / s
   # cap? max <=1, but if one value 1, ok. If due to inf cleaned.
   return a
```
We may want to keep tiny zeros, so positives only. For exact candidate, if all zero, fallback.

   # scale to sum 1 if possible

       arr=arr/arr.sum() # sum maybe <0.01, scale up; if sum 0 fallback
   else: arr=full(1,? n=arr.size or 512)
s=arr.sum()
if s <=0: arr=arr? 
# We want sum=1 for scale invariance. If s>0, arr=arr/s
return arr

```
def _optimize_block(q0, n, mode, rng, end_time, lp_iters, grad_steps):
    q = _normalize(q0)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_s = _score_norm(q, mode)
    def upd(qc, sc): nonlocal ...
    # LP phase
    if linprog is not None and lp_iters>0:
        stagn=0
        for it in range(lp_iters):
            if time.monotonic() > end_time - 10: break
            lp_end = min(end_time - 5, time.monotonic() + 30.0)
            if lp_end < time.monotonic()+1: break
            g = _lp_response(q, mode, n, rng, lp_end)
            if g is None:
                break
            # line search
            sc_best=best_s; q_best=best_q; accept=False
            for t in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0):
                cand = (1-t)*q + t*g
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                sc = _score_norm(cand, mode)
                if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s=sc; best_q=cand.copy(); q=cand; accept=True
            if accept:
                q=best_q; best_s=...; stagn=0
                # periodically update global? outside
            else:
                stagn +=1
                # maybe try direct g
                if _score_norm(g,mode) < best_s -1e-12: q=g; ...
                if stagn >= 4: break
    # Gradient phase
    # if best_s near lower? For mode full lower 2/(2n?)? score lower 2; if best_s <= 2 + 1e-9 return. But internal normalized score lower 2.
    # If off no.
    if mode=='full' and best_s <= 2.0 + 1e-9:
        return best_q,best_s
    # Gradient
    if grad_steps>0 and time.time()<end_time-2:
       # tau based on bmax
       bmax = best_s/(2*n) if best_s>0 else 1e-12
       tau = max(1e-12, 0.5*bmax)
       stag=0
       for it in range(grad_steps):
          if time.time()>end_time-1: break
          grad,bm = _gradient(q,mode,tau)
          if not finite or bm<=0: break
          d = -(grad - grad.mean())
          gn = np.linalg.norm(d)
          if gn <= 1e-14: break
          unit = d/gn
          improved=False
          # try steps normalized and eta by grad magnitude
          base_steps = [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0]
          for c in base_steps:
             cand = q + c*unit
             cand = _normalize(cand)
             sc = _score_norm(cand, mode)
             if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                 best_s=sc; best_q=cand.copy(); q=cand; improved=True
          # also random direction occasionally
          if (it+1)%25==0:
             r = rng.normal(size=n); r-=r.mean(); rn=np.linalg.norm(r); if rn>0:
                for c in [0.01,0.03,0.1]:
                   cand = q + c*(r/rn)
                   ...
          if improved:
             stag=0
             # maybe adjust tau up if stuck? if local? keep
          else:
             stag +=1
             tau *= 1.3
             if stag >= 20:
                 # random restart around best? q = normalize(best_q + small noise)
                 r = rng.normal(size=n); r-=r.mean(); q=_normalize(best_q + 0.02*(r/np.linalg.norm(r))); best_s=_score_norm(q,mode)?? This might worsen. Use only if random improves? Could skip.
                 tau = max(1e-12, 0.2*bmax)
          tau = max(1e-15, tau*0.995)
          if mode=='full' and best_s <= 2+1e-10: break
    # final exact consider outside
    return best_q, best_s
```

Let's design `_exact_gradient(q, use_center=True)`:
```

   # Use one-sided when q_i < h.
   h = 1e-5
   # To keep nonnegative, plus h <=? no cap, minus needs q_i>h.
   # We can choose h = min(1e-4, max(1e-7, 0.5*q[i]))
```
But if q_i=0, central not possible. We can use one-sided plus and base: derivative approx (F(q+h)-F(base))/h. For near zero, gradient. For plus, after normalize sum preserved by subtract mean? q+h then subtract mean (h/n). For minus, if q_i < h, maybe skip or use h = q_i*0.5.
Better: Use basis direction v_i = e_i - 1/n 1. Choose step δ_i such that q_i ± δ_i > -small. δ_i = min(1e-4, max(1e-7, q_i*0.5)) for minus; plus can be same. Then:
```
cand_plus = q.copy(); cand_plus += δ_i * v_i
cand_minus = q - δ_i * v_i

def _safe_exact(arr):
    try:
       lst = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
       if not lst: return inf
       val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
       val = float(val)
       return val if np.isfinite(val) else inf
    except Exception:
       return inf
```

`_clean_for_eval(a)`:
```
def _clean_for_eval(a, default_n=512):
   arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: arr=np.full(default_n, 1.0/default_n)
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr) & (arr >= 0.0), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       return np.full(arr.size if arr.size>0 else default_n, 1.0/(arr.size if arr.size>0 else default_n))
   arr = arr / s
   return arr
```
This always sum=1. If some values negative clipped. Cap after scale? Sum=1 ensures max≤1, no cap active. If input had huge values, sum huge, after scale max maybe <=1? If one value huge and others small, after sum 1 max <1. Good.

`_normalize_internal(a, default_n=512)` similar but maybe doesn't need cap; returns sum=1. It clips >0 only? For internal we may clip negative. Use same but excludes zero? `np.where(finite & (a>0), a,0)`. Fine.

For performance, in inner optimization, q normalized sum1; score can compute b and max. Use no sum division.

```
def _score_norm(q, mode):
    n=q.size
    if n==0: return inf
    b=np.convolve(q,q)
    if mode=='off' and n>1:
        # b indices 0..n-2 and n..2n-2
        mb = -np.inf
        if n-1 > 0:
            mb = max(mb, float(np.max(b[:n-1])))
        if b.size > n:
            mb = max(mb, float(np.max(b[n:])))
        if not np.isfinite(mb): return inf
    else:
        mb = float(np.max(b))
    val = 2.0*n*mb
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else inf
```
For n=1 off, use full: mb=b[0].

Gradient as above.

LP response:
```
def _lp_response(q, mode, n, rng, time_limit):
    if linprog is None: return None
    L=2*n-1
    b=np.convolve(q,q)
    if mode=='off' and n>1:
       rhs = np.empty(L)
       offmax = max of b off
       rhs[:] = offmax
       rhs[n-1] = b[n-1]
    else:
       m = float(np.max(b)); rhs = np.full(L, m)
    scale = float(n)
    # Build A
    A = np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    qs = q * scale
    for l in range(L):
       s = l - (n-1)
       if s < 0: s = 0
       e = l
       if e >= n: e = n-1
       if s <= e:
           A[l, s:e+1] = qs[l-e : l-s+1][::-1]
    b_ub = rhs * scale
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # small random / anti-align
    c += 1e-7 * rng.normal(size=n)
    c += 1e-5 * (q - q.mean())  # positive where q larger, less preferred
    # Maybe ensure c not positive? It can be positive if q mean? q positive, c base -1, small no issue.
    try:
       try:
          res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, 20.0), method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': float(max(1.0, time_limit))})
       except Exception:
          res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, 20.0), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False):
       x = getattr(res,'x',None)
       if x is None: return None
       # maybe optimal?
    else: x=res.x
    g = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g)&(g>0), g, 0.0)
    s=g.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    g = g/s
    return g
```
But setting time_limit inside may raise if not accepted. Use options `{'presolve': True}` only; rely on outer time. Because HiGHS may not stop fast? It will. To avoid options error, only presolve. Maybe `time_limit` supported but no need.

If LP presolve with bounds (0,20) and infeasible? feasible q with q max≤1≤20, yes.

LP all constraints can be numerous. For off mode with rhs zero for offmax=0, q feasible? If q delta, off b zero, rhs off 0, zero rhs 1. A q = n*conv(q,q) has off 0, zero n. b_ub off 0, zero n. feasible. Good. If offmax very small, constraints tight.

Now, exact gradient helper:
```
def _exact_gradient(q, default_n):
   n=q.size
   baseE = _evaluate_clean(_normalize_internal(q)) # maybe q already clean
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # scale h: base = max(1e-5, 1e-4 * np.median(q[q>0]?))
   h_abs = 1e-5
   for i in range(n):
      # choose δ = min(1e-4, max(1e-7, 0.4*q[i]))
      delta = min(1e-4, max(1e-7, 0.4 * q[i] if q[i]>0 else 1e-7))
      v = -1.0/ n  # for all
      v_pos = 1.0 - 1.0/n
      cand_p = q.copy(); cand_m = q.copy()
      cand_p[i] += delta * v_pos; cand_p -= delta/(n) ? Wait v = e_i - 1/n ones, so entries others subtract delta/n; entry i add delta*(1-1/n)? If v_i = e_i - (1/n)1, component i = 1 - 1/n, others = -1/n. To keep sum 0 exactly. Use this.
      cand_p = q + delta * v
      cand_m = q - delta * v
      # To avoid negative, if min cand_m < 0, reduce delta:
      if cand_m.min() < 0:
          # max feasible delta for minus:
          # entry i: q_i - delta*(1-1/n) >=0 => δ <= q_i/(1-1/n); others: q_j + δ/n? since minus v = -e_i + 1/n, others +δ/n, so only entry i can go negative.
          maxδ = q[i] / max(1e-12, 1.0 - 1.0/n)
          delta = max(1e-10, 0.5 * min(delta, maxδ))
          cand_p = q + delta*v; cand_m = q - delta*v
      # If still negative due to min? clip
      cand_p = np.where(cand_p>0, cand_p, 0.0); cand_m = ...
      cand_p = _normalize_internal(cand_p)
      cand_m = _normalize_internal(cand_m)
      Ep = _evaluate_clean(cand_p); Em = _evaluate_clean(cand_m)
      if finite both and delta>0: grad[i] = (Ep-Em)/(2*delta)
   grad -= grad.mean()
   return grad
```
Wait if v_i = e_i - 1/n ones, change in coordinate i is delta*(1-1/n) ~delta. approximating derivative with denominator 2*delta is okay aside factor. Since we only use direction, scaling irrelevant. Good.
However, `_normalize_internal` after perturbation may alter the magnitude if clipping occurs. But small delta no clip if q_i positive enough. For q_i zero, v plus increases, minus decreases i by ~delta to negative, clip to 0, not symmetric; derivative one-sided. We can for zero use one-sided: F(q+δv) - F(q-? cannot). The formula with clip may still produce a directional derivative. Good.

Exact polish:
```
def _exact_polish(q, time_end, max_iters=200):
    q = _normalize_internal(q)
    best = q.copy(); bestE = _evaluate_clean(best)
    no_improve = 0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > time_end - 5: break
       # if residual time too small for gradient (e.g. <3s), break
       grad = _exact_gradient(best, default_n=best.size)
       d = -grad; d -= d.mean(); gn=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if gn < 1e-12: break
       unit = d/gn
       improved=False
       for c in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0):
           cand = best + c*unit
           cand = _normalize_internal(cand)
           sc = _evaluate_clean(cand)
           if sc < bestE - 1e-12:
               bestE=sc; best=cand.copy(); improved=True
       if improved: no_improve=0
       else:
           # try negative unit? d already negative gradient; if noisy maybe unit sign? Try -unit with small c?
           for c in (0.002,0.005,0.01):
              cand = best + c*(-unit); ...
           if not improved: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve >= 3: break
    return best, bestE
```
But if exact gradient has sign error due to finite diff denominator, unit maybe opposite. We try both signs. Good.

Pseudo `_optimize_start`:
```
def _optimize_start(q0,...):
   q = _normalize_internal(q0)
   best_q=q.copy(); best_s=_score_norm(q,mode)
   if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
   # LP
   if linprog and lp_iters>0:
      stagn=0
      for _ in range(lp_iters):
         if time.monotonic() > end_time - 20: break # leave gradient?
         max_lp_time = min(25.0, max(1.0, (end_time - time.monotonic() - 10)/float(max(1,lp_iters - _))))? 
         lp_end = time.monotonic() + min(25.0, max(1, ...))
         if lp_end > end_time - 10: lp_end = end_time - 10
         g = _lp_response(q,mode,q.size,rng,lp_end - now)
         if g is None:
             # maybe stop LP if persistent
             break
         improved=False
         for t in ...:
            cand=(1-t)*q+t*g; cand=_normalize_internal(cand); sc=_score_norm(cand,mode)
            if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s=sc; best_q=cand.copy(); q=cand; improved=True
         if improved:
             if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
             stagn=0
         else:
             stagn +=1
             if stagn>=4: break
   # Gradient
   if grad_iters>0:
       # if mode full and best_s <=2+1e-9 return
       tau = max(1e-12, 0.5 * (best_s/(2*n)) if best_s>0 else 1e-12)
       no=0
       for it in range(grad_iters):
          if time.monotonic() > end_time - 3: break
          if mode=='full' and best_s <= 2+1e-10: break
          grad,bm = _gradient(q,mode,tau)
          d = -(grad - grad.mean()); gn=norm
          if gn <=1e-14: break
          unit=d/gn; improved=False
          for c in (...):
             cand = q + c*unit; cand=_normalize_internal(cand); sc=_score_norm(cand,mode)
             if sc < best_s - 1e-12: ... improved True
          # random move every 20
          if (it%20==19):
             r = rng.normal(n); r-=r.mean(); rn=norm; if rn>0:
                 r/=rn
                 for c in (0.005,0.02,0.08): ...
          if improved:
             no=0
             if consider_func and it%5==0: consider_func(best_q)
          else:
             no +=1
             tau *= 1.2
             if no >= 15:
                # reset q to best small random? maybe
                break
          tau = max(1e-15, tau*0.99)
       if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
   return best_q,best_s
```
Need be careful: q may not equal best_q if line search only accepted best; we set q=best. Good.

```
def _upscale(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a).ravel(); if size==0 return full
   if size==n: return _normalize_internal(a)
   if size==1: return full(n,1/n)
   if n % size ==0: out=np.repeat(a, n//size)
   else:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,size); y=np.linspace(0,1,n); out=np.interp(y,x,a)
   return _normalize_internal(out)
```

def try_candidates(cands):
    nonlocal q, best_q, best_s, best_e
    scored=[]
    ex_best = best_e if exact_accept else inf
    for cand_set in cands:
        c = _normalize_internal(cand_set)
        fs = _score_norm(c, mode)
        # if exact_accept: maybe compute exact only if fs is competitive or for all small number
        scored.append((fs, c))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    # evaluate exact for top min(4, len)
    top = scored[:min(4, len(scored))]
    for fs, c in top:
        if exact_accept:
            e = _evaluate_clean(c)
            if e < ex_best - 1e-12:
                ex_best = e; best_c = c; best_ex = e
        else:
            if fs < best_s - ...: ...
    if improved: update q,best
```

Let's write `_optimize_guided` top-level:
```
def _optimize_guided(q0, n, mode, rng, end_time, lp_iters, grad_iters, consider_func=None):
    q = _normalize_internal(q0)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_e = _evaluate_clean(best_q)
    best_f = _score_norm(best_q, mode)
    if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
    if not np.isfinite(best_e):
        # fallback use fast only? set best_e = best_f
        best_e = best_f
    tol=1e-12
    def try_point(cand):
       nonlocal ...
       c=_normalize_internal(cand)
       e=_evaluate_clean(c)
       f=_score_norm(c,mode)
       if (np.isfinite(e) and (e < best_e - tol or (abs(e-best_e)<=tol and f < best_f - 1e-10))):
           best_e=e; best_q=c.copy(); best_f=f; q=c; if consider_func: consider_func(best_q); return True
       return False
```
But assigning `q=c` inside nested try_point requires nonlocal q. Python nonlocal for q,best_q,best_e,best_f.

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def _normalize_internal(a, default_n=512):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(max(1, default_n), 1.0/max(1, default_n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(1,a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    return a
```
If a.size large default? okay.

`_clean_for_eval` similar but for candidate:
```
def _clean_for_eval(a, default_n=512):
   arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n = arr.size if arr.size>0 else default_n
   if arr.size==0:
       arr=np.full(n,1.0/n)
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr) & (arr >=0.0), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if not finite or s<=0:
       arr=np.full(arr.size, 1.0/max(1,arr.size))
   else:
       arr=arr / s
   return arr
```
Since scale to 1, cap no issue. But if input length is 1 and value 0? arr zeros, s=0 fallback 1.

`_score_mode` for arrays not necessarily sum1:
```
def _score_mode(a, mode='full'):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    if mode=='off' and n>1:
       mb = -np.inf
       if n-1 > 0: mb=max(mb,float(np.max(b[:n-1])))
       if b.size > n: mb=max(mb,float(np.max(b[n:])))
       if not finite: return inf
    else:
       mb = float(np.max(b))
    val = 2.0*n*mb/(s*s)
    return val if finite else inf
```
For n=1 off: use full.

`_score_norm(q,mode)`:
```
def _score_norm(q,mode='full'):
   q=np.asarray(q,dtype=float).ravel(); n=q.size
   if n==0: return inf
   b=np.convolve(q,q)
   if mode=='off' and n>1:
      mb=-np.inf
      if n-1>0: mb=max(mb,float(np.max(b[:n-1])))
      if b.size>n: mb=max(mb,float(np.max(b[n:])))
      if not finite: return inf
   else: mb=float(np.max(b))
   val=2.0*n*mb
   return val if finite else inf
```

`_upscale`:
```
def _upscale(a, n, default_n=512):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   n=int(max(1,n))
   if a.size==0:
      return _normalize_internal(np.ones(n), n)
   if a.size == n:
      return _normalize_internal(a,n)
   if a.size == 1:
      return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   if n % a.size == 0:
      out = np.repeat(a, n//a.size)
   else:
      x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
      y=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
      out=np.interp(y,x,a)
   return _normalize_internal(out,n)
```
For parent upscaling from 512 to 512, returns normalize.

def _optimize_guided(...):
    q = _normalize_internal(q0,n)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_e = _evaluate_clean(best_q)
    best_f = _score_norm(best_q, mode)
    if not np.isfinite(best_e): best_e = best_f
    if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
    tol=1e-12
    radius = 1.0 / max(1.0, np.sqrt(max(1,n)))
    step_mults = (0.05,0.15,0.5,1.5,5.0,15.0)
    neg_mults = (0.05,0.15)

       stagn=0

          if now > end_time - 15: break
          lp_time = min(30.0, (end_time - now - 5)/float(max(1, lp_iters-it)))
          lp_time = max(0.5, lp_time)

          local_e=best_e; local_q=best_q; local_f=best_f; improved=False

`_exact_gradient` implementation as discussed. Need use `_evaluate_clean`.
```
def _exact_gradient(q):
   q = _normalize_internal(q)
   n=q.size
   if n <=1: return np.zeros(n)
   base_e = _evaluate_clean(q)
   if not finite: return zeros
   inv=1.0/n
   ones=1.0-inv
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # choose delta scale maybe based on q median: qmean=1/n
   base_h = 1e-4
   for i in range(n):
      # adapt delta to coordinate value
      qi = float(q[i])
      if qi > 0:
         delta = min(1e-4, max(1e-7, 0.5*qi))
      else:
         delta = 1e-6
      # maximum for minus central:
      max_minus = qi / ones if qi > 0 else 0.0
      if delta*ones > max_minus:
           # one-sided
           delta = max(1e-8, 0.5*min(delta, max_minus if max_minus>0 else 1e-6))
           cand = q.copy(); cand[i] += delta*ones; cand -= delta*inv
           cand = np.where(cand>0,cand,0.0); cand=_normalize_internal(cand)
           e = _evaluate_clean(cand)
           if finite e and base_e finite and delta>0:
              grad[i] = (e - base_e)/delta
           continue
      cand_p = q.copy(); cand_p[i] += delta*ones; cand_p -= delta*inv
      cand_m = q.copy(); cand_m[i] -= delta*ones; cand_m += delta*inv
      # if due to rounding negative clip
      if cand_p.min() < 0 or cand_m.min() < 0:
          # reduce delta and retry simple
          delta2 = max(1e-10, 0.5*min(delta, max_minus))
          cand_p = q.copy(); cand_p[i] += delta2*ones; cand_p -= delta2*inv
          cand_m = q.copy(); cand_m[i] -= delta2*ones; cand_m += delta2*inv
          delta = delta2
      cand_p = np.where(cand_p>0,cand_p,0.0); cand_p=_normalize_internal(cand_p)
      cand_m = np.where(cand_m>0,cand_m,0.0); cand_m=_normalize_internal(cand_m)
      ep=_evaluate_clean(cand_p); em=_evaluate_clean(cand_m)
      if finite ep and finite em and delta>0:
          grad[i] = (ep - em) / (2*delta)
   grad -= np.mean(grad)
   return grad
```
This central difference derivative with denominator 2δ but coordinate change is δ*ones; okay direction. Could under/over scale. If gradient components have different scale due to coords? It is derivative along basis v_i; good.
Potential issue: If exact evaluator has clamp, q normalized; perturbing all entries by -δ/n may make some zero entries negative for many coordinates? For a coordinate i, cand_m: all other entries decrease by δ/n. If some other entry is very small (< δ/n), clipping may bias. To avoid, choose δ small relative to minimum positive entry? δ=1e-4, n=512 -> δ/n=2e-7, smaller than min positive maybe. If q has tiny entries 1e-9, clipping possible. But scale 1, typical uniform 0.002. For sparse, zeros for others unaffected? If other zero, cand_m adds +δ/n (since v_i: others -1/n, minus v_i gives +1/n), so zeros become positive, no negative. Only q_i can go negative. Good. For plus, others decrease; if others zero, negative by -δ/n, clipped; this introduces a global scaling? For q_i=0, plus direction adds to i and subtracts tiny from all others, including zeros -> negative clipped. For sparse, this perturbation moves mass from all positives and zero negatives (clipped) then normalize. It's okay one-sided. Exact polish:
```
def _exact_polish(q, end_time, max_iters=50):
   q=_normalize_internal(q); n=q.size
   best=q.copy(); best_e=_evaluate_clean(best)
   if best_e <= 1e-12: return best,best_e
   radius=1/np.sqrt(n)
   noimp=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic() > end_time - 3: break
      grad=_exact_gradient(best)
      d=-grad; d-=d.mean(); gn=np.linalg.norm(d)
      if not finite or gn<1e-14: break
      unit=d/gn
      base=best; local_e=best_e; local_q=best
      # try directions: unit, -unit, random couple
      dirs = [unit]
      if it > 5: dirs.append(-unit)
      for _ in range(2): dirs.append(random unit)
      for dir in dirs:
         for mult in (0.05,0.15,0.5,1.5,5.0):
            cand=base+(mult*radius)*dir; cand=normalize; e=eval; if e<local_e: local...
      if local_e < best_e - 1e-12:
          best=local_q.copy(); best_e=local_e; noimp=0
      else:
          noimp+=1
          if noimp>=4:
              # random kick around best
              for mult in (0.5,1.5,3.0):
                 v=random; cand=best+(mult*radius)*v; if improves update
              if not improved: break
   return best,best_e
```

```
def _detect_mode(probe_arrays, default_n):
    full_d=0; off_d=0; cnt=0; below=False
    for a in probe_arrays:
       if a is None: continue
       c=_clean_for_eval(a)
       if c.size==0: continue
       e=_safe_exact(c)
       f=_score_mode(c,'full')
       o=_score_mode(c,'off')
       if finite all:
          if e < 2.0 - 1e-6: below=True
          full_d += abs(e-f); off_d += abs(e-o); cnt+=1
    if cnt==0: return 'full'
    if below and off_d < full_d - 1e-4: return 'off'
    return 'full'
```

- Use projected gradient on simplex (sum1,q≥0). Direction = - (grad - mean(grad)). Because φ shifts invariant? The denominator sum fixed; moving on tangent. Normalize.

```

`_smoothed_grad(q, tau)` full all constraints:
```
n=q.size; b=np.convolve(q,q); bmax=b.max(); if bmax<=0 return zeros, bmax
x=(b-bmax)/tau; p=np.exp(x-x.max()); p/=p.sum()
# optional if tau large p uniform; direct conv
h = np.convolve(q, p[::-1], 'full')
grad = 2.0 * h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
return grad,bmax
```
Check h slice: For n=512, p[::-1] length 1023, q length512, full length1533. h[n-1:2n-1] -> indices 511 to 1022 inclusive length512. Reversed. Good. Let's test uniform: p weights maybe centered; grad maybe symmetric. Good.

```

`_polish_gradient(q, max_time, max_iters, rng)`:
```
q=_project; score=...
tau = 0.5 * (score/(2*n)) # bmax
tau_min = 1e-8 * bmax maybe 1e-10
stall=0
for it in range(max_iters):
  if time > max_time: break
  grad,bmax = _smoothed_grad(q,tau)
  d = -(grad - grad.mean())
  dn=norm(d)
  if dn < 1e-14: break
  unit=d/dn
  best_c=q; best_s=score
  radius=1/sqrt(n)
  for mult in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0):
      cand = q + (mult*radius)*unit
      cand_project; sc=score
      if sc<best_s: ...
  # also maybe negative direction if unit sign weird? Since d=-grad_centered, should decrease. But numerical maybe; we can include -unit with small multiples every 10.
  if best_s < score - 1e-13:
      q=best_c; score=best_s; stall=0
      # adjust tau: if improved a lot, maybe reduce? Keep.
  else:
      stall +=1
      # adapt tau: if stuck, blur to find direction? increase tau
      tau = min(tau * 1.5, 0.5)
      if stall > 30:
          # random restart small kick
          v=rng.normal(n); v-=v.mean(); v/=norm
          for mult in (0.1,0.3,1.0):
             cand=q+(mult*radius)*v; if score < best? accept
          if not improved: break

return q,score
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
   t0=time.monotonic(); headroom=25; deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s-headroom)
   rng=default_rng
   best_list=None; best_score=inf
   DEFAULT_N=512 (if parent size)
   parent = globals().get...
   def consider(a): ... evaluate exact, update if < best_score
   # initial candidates
   unif = _norm(np.ones(DEFAULT_N)); consider(unif)
   if parent: consider(parent)
   base4, cosine, block ... consider
   if time left < 20: finalize
   # define optimize function with consider callback? It returns q, score; we consider outside.
   # coarse schedule
   if time left:
       lengths = [16,32,64,128,256]
       weights = [0.5,1,2,4,8]
       remaining_for_coarse = min( (deadline - now) * 0.30, 300.0 )? Need leave 512.
       total_w = sum(weights) + main_w? Maybe compute:
          total_search = deadline - now
          coarse_share = max(0.0, min(350.0, total_search*0.30))
          main_share = total_search - coarse_share
          coarse_alloc = weights/sum(weights)*coarse_share
       prev=None
       for k,alloc in ...:
          if alloc < 3: continue
          start_time=now; end=start+alloc
          starts=[]
          if parent: starts.append(_upscale(parent, k, 'interp'))
          if prev: starts.append(_upscale(prev,k,'repeat'))
          starts.append(_norm(np.ones(k)))
          starts.append(_cosine_start(k,rng,phase=...))
          # maybe tile
          # Run each start with alloc/num_starts, but ensure at least one long.
          best_q_local=None; best_s_local=inf
          for q0 in starts:
             sub_end = min(end, now + max(2, (end-now)/len(starts)))
             q_opt,s_opt = _optimize(q0, k, rng, sub_end, lp..., grad...)
             consider(q_opt); update local
          if local: prev=local
   # 512 stage
   if time left > 60:
      main_n = DEFAULT_N (512)
      starts=[]
      if parent: parent_u = _upscale(parent, main_n, 'interp') (if same normalize)
      if prev: prev_u = _upscale(prev, main_n, 'repeat'/'interp')
      starts append parent, prev, cosine, random, maybe uniform.
      # unique by maybe not needed
      remaining = deadline - now
      # allocate 60% to best start (parent if exists else prev), 30% second, 10% third.
      # Sort starts by exact score? We can evaluate exact for each candidate and sort by score ascending.
      scored = [(exact_score(q), q) for q in starts]
      sort
      for i,(sc,q) in enumerate(scored[:4]):
          alloc = remaining * fracs [0.55,0.25,0.12,0.08] for first four
          end=now+alloc; if end < now+5 break
          q_opt,s_opt = _optimize(q, main_n, rng, end, lp_iters=12, grad_iters...)
          consider(q_opt)
   # final: if time left, run exact finite-difference? Not necessary but can. Maybe a few random perturbations of best around target.
   finalize validate
```

`_cosine_start(n, rng, freq_choices?)`:
```
t=np.arange(n)
raw=np.zeros(n)
for m in range(1, 7):
   amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)/m
   phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
   raw += amp*np.cos(2π*m*t/n + phase)
raw += 1.0
raw = np.maximum(raw, 1e-6)
return _normalize(raw)
```

Now, exact evaluator and fast._score_fast for normalized arrays:
```
def _score_fast(q):
   n=q.size; b=np.convolve(q,q); mb=b.max(); s=q.sum(); if s<=0 inf; val=2*n*mb/(s*s); return val
```
In line search, we normalize q sum1; use `2n*mb` to save sum.

`_project(q, n)`:
```
q = np.where(np.isfinite(q) & (q > 0.0), q, 0.0)
s=q.sum()
if s<=0: return np.full(n,1/n)
return q/s
```
This clips negatives after step.

`_smooth_grad(q, tau)` with full all.
Need handle if bmax=0 (impossible). If tau too small, p may underflow; use `x=(b-bmax)/tau; mx=x.max(); p=np.exp(x-mx)`. Since mx=0, but due to b-bmax negative. `p_sum`; if 0, p uniform over b==bmax.

`_gradient_polish` in `_optimize`:
```
def _gradient_polish(q, end_time, rng, iters_max, consider_func=None):
    n=q.size; q=_project(q,n)
    best=q.copy(); best_s=_score_norm(best)
    bmax=best_s/(2*n)
    tau=max(1e-12, 0.25*bmax)
    no=0
    radius=1.0/np.sqrt(n)
    step_mults=(0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0)
    for it in range(iters_max):
       if time.monotonic() > end_time: break
       grad,bmax=_smooth_grad(q,tau)
       d=-(grad-grad.mean()); dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if dn < 1e-15: break
       unit=d/dn
       base=q; local_s=best_s; local_q=best
       for mult in step_mults:
           if time.monotonic() > end_time: break
           cand = base + mult*radius*unit
           cand = _project(cand,n)
           s = _score_norm(cand)
           if s < local_s - 1e-12:
               local_s=s; local_q=cand.copy()
       # random direction every 20 or if no improve 5
       if (it % 20 == 19) or (local_s == best_s and it>5):
           for _ in range(2):
              v=rng.normal(n); v-=v.mean(); vn=np.linalg.norm(v)
              if vn>0:
                 v/=vn
                 for mult in (0.03,0.1,0.3):
                    cand=base+mult*radius*v; cand=_project; s=_score_norm
                    if s < local_s - 1e-12: ...
       if local_s < best_s - 1e-12:
           best=local_q; best_s=local_s; q=best.copy()
           no=0
           # consider every 10
           if consider_func and (it % 10 == 0): consider_func(best)
       else:
           no +=1
           # adapt tau
           tau *= 1.2
           tau = min(tau, 0.5*bmax if bmax>0 else 1e-8)
           if no >= 40:
               # random kick from best
               v=...
               kick=False
               for mult in (0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
                   cand=best+mult*radius*v; proj; s=score
                   if s < best_s:
                      best=cand; best_s=s; q=best.copy(); kick=True; no=0; if consider...
               if not kick: break
       tau *= 0.995
       tau = max(tau, 1e-12)
       # lower bound check: theoretical min >= 2n/(2n-1)
       if best_s <= 2.0*n/(2*n-1) + 1e-9: break
    if consider_func: consider_func(best)
    return best,best_s
```
Potential problem: step_mults up to 10*radius ~0.44; for n=16 radius 0.25, max 2.5 huge; but projection/clip okay. Could adapt radius*5 max. fine.

We can compute full convolution using `np.fft.rfft`:
```
def _convolve(a):
   n=a.size
   if n <= 512: return np.convolve(a,a)
   size = 1; while size < 2*n-1: size *=2
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
```
This is O(n log n) and accurate enough. Use for all? For n=512, FFT may be faster? overhead, but okay. Could use threshold 512. This will drastically speed 1024/2048 gradient. ```
def _conv_square(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 1024:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    m=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size < m: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    return c[:m]
```
For n=2048, size 4096, fast.

def _conv(a,b):
   if a.size==0 or b.size==0: return np.empty(0)
   if a.size*b.size <= 2_000_000: return np.convolve(a,b)
   m=a.size+b.size-1; size=1<<(m-1).bit_length(); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size); return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:m]
```

```
def _optimize(q0, n, rng, end_time, lp_iters=10, consider_func=None):
    q = _project(q0, n)
    best_q = q.copy(); best_s = _score_norm(q)
    if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
    # LP
    if linprog is None: use_lp=False
    elif lp_iters>0 and (end_time - now) > 15 and n <= 2048:
       lp_end = min(end_time, now + max(5.0, 0.25*(end_time-now)))
       stagn=0
       for it in range(lp_iters):
          if now > lp_end - 8: break
          remaining_loops = max(1, lp_iters-it)
          tlim = min(60.0, max(1.0, (lp_end - now - 2)/float(remaining_loops)))
          if tlim < 0.5: break
          g = _lp_response(q, n, rng, tlim)
          if g is None:
             break
          base=q; local_s=best_s; local_q=best_q
          for t in ...:
             cand=(1-t)*base+t*g; cand=_project; s=_score_norm
             if s<local_s: local...
          if local_s < best_s - 1e-12:
             best_s=local_s; best_q=local_q.copy(); q=best_q.copy(); stagn=0
             if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
          else:
             stagn += 1
             if stagn >= 4: break
    # gradient
    if end_time - now > 3:
       q,best_s = _gradient_polish(q,best_s,end_time,rng,consider_func)
    if consider_func: consider_func(best_q)
    return best_q,best_s
```
But `_gradient_polish` needs initial best_s. If no LP, q unchanged.

In gradient, after each accepted, `best_q` in `_optimize` should update? We can have `_gradient_polish` returns new best. Good.

`_gradient_polish` details with mirror steps:
```
def _gradient_polish(q, best_s, end_time, rng, consider_func=None):
    n=q.size; radius=1/np.sqrt(n)
    tau = max(1e-12, 0.25 * (best_s/(2*n)))
    no=0
    for it in range(1000000):
       if time.monotonic() > end_time: break
       grad,bmax = _smooth_grad(q,tau)
       if not finite: break
       d = -(grad - grad.mean()); dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
       # Euclidean candidates
       base_loc=None; local_s=best_s; local_q=q
       if dn > 1e-14:
          unit=d/dn
          for mult in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
             cand = q + mult*radius*unit
             cand = _project(cand,n); s=_score_norm(cand)
             if s < local_s - 1e-12: local...
       # mirror candidates
       g = grad - grad.mean()
       maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if maxg > 1e-14:
          for mult in (0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0):
             # to avoid underflow, exponent = -mult*g/maxg (range -mult..mult, if mult30 maybe exp(-30) underflow okay)
             scale = q * np.exp(- (mult / maxg) * g)
             scale = np.where(finite & (scale>0), scale, 0)
             cand = _project(scale,n)
             s = _score_norm(cand)
             if s < local_s-...: local...
       # random every 20
       if it % 20 == 19 or local_s == best_s:
          for _ in range(2):
             v=...; for mult in (0.03,0.1,0.3): ...
       if local_s < best_s - 1e-12:
          best_s=local_s; q=local_q.copy(); no=0
          if consider_func and (improvement >1e-6 or it%25==0): consider_func(q)
       else:
          no +=1
          tau *= 1.25
          if no >= 30:
             # random kick from best q
             v=...
             kick=False
             for mult in (0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
                 cand=q+mult*radius*v; if score < best_s: update; kick=True; no=0
             if not kick: break
       tau *= 0.99
       tau = max(tau, 1e-12)
       if best_s <= 2.0*n/(2*n-1) + 1e-9: break
    if consider_func: consider_func(q)
    return q, best_s
```
Need `best_q` not separately; return q. `consider_func` called at end.

The lower bound `2n/(2n-1)` is average max; but score maybe can be slightly below due to floating? It's a lower bound: max b ≥ 1/(2n-1); score ≥ 2n/(2n-1). For n=512 ~1.002. So break if reach near. Not near target.

Potential issue: Mirror update with g where some entries zero: exp factor finite; if q_i zero, stays zero. If start has zeros, cannot move mass into zero unless Euclidean/random adds. Our starts mostly positive (floor). Good. We can ensure starts positive floor: `_project` sets zeros but if constructed with +1e-6 all positive. For uniform/parent maybe zeros? Parent likely positive? Could be zeros. We can add floor in `_project`? If we add floor always, exact score changes slightly but improves search. But final candidate with tiny positive all entries valid. Does floor hurt score? Sum1, floor 1e-8 maybe negligible. We can use `_project_floor(q, n, floor=1e-8)`? If an optimal has zeros, floor could slightly increase score near target? The coefficient cap no. 1e-8 relative tiny; score change ~1e-8. Could use floor 1e-10. ```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _normalize_internal(a, default_n=512):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        n = max(1, int(default_n))
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _clean_for_eval(a, default_n=512):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        n = max(1, int(default_n))
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a >= 0.0), a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _safe_exact(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        v = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        v = float(v)
        return v if np.isfinite(v) else float("inf")
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _evaluate_clean(a, default_n=512):
    try:
        return _safe_exact(_clean_for_eval(a, default_n))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _conv_any(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size * b.size <= 2_000_000:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    m = a.size + b.size - 1
    size = 1 << max(0, (m - 1).bit_length())
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)[:m]


def _conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 700:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << max(0, (m - 1).bit_length())
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return c[:m]


def _score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    b = _conv_square(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    val = 2.0 * n * mb / (s * s)
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def _score_norm(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = _conv_square(q)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * n * mb


def _project_simplex(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size != n:
        v = np.resize(v, n)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v) & (v > 0.0), v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def _smoothed_grad(q, tau):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    b = _conv_square(q)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    bmax = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        tau = max(1e-12, 0.25 * bmax)

    x = (b - bmax) / tau
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        p = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        k = int(np.argmax(x))
        p[k] = 1.0
    else:
        p = np.exp(x - mx)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
            k = int(np.argmax(b))
            p[k] = 1.0
        else:
            p /= ps

    h = _conv_any(q, p[::-1])
    if h.size < 2 * n - 1:
        pfull = np.zeros(3 * n - 2, dtype=np.float64)
        pfull[:p.size] = p
        h = _conv_any(q, pfull[::-1])
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h) & (h > 0.0), h, 0.0)

    grad = 2.0 * h[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad, bmax


def _upscale(a, n, mode="interp"):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a >= 0.0), a, 0.0)
    if a.size == n:
        return _normalize_internal(a, n)

    if mode == "repeat" and n % a.size == 0:
        out = np.repeat(a, n // a.size)
    elif mode == "repeat":
        k = max(1, n // a.size)
        out = np.repeat(a, k)
        if out.size < n:
            out = np.concatenate([out, a[: n - out.size]])
        out = out[:n]
        if out.size < n:
            out = np.resize(out, n)
    else:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(y, x, a)

    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out) & (out > 0.0), out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def _bell_start(n, center=True, rng=None):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if center:
        shape = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (x - 0.5))
    else:
        shape = 0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (x - 0.5))
    if rng is not None:
        shape = shape + 0.03 * rng.normal(size=n)
    raw = 0.05 + 0.95 * np.where(np.isfinite(shape) & (shape > 0.0), shape, 0.0)
    return _normalize_internal(raw, n)


def _cosine_start(n, rng):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    raw = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxm = max(3, min(12, int(np.log2(n)) + 3))
    for m in range(1, maxm + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75) / float(m)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        raw += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t / float(n) + ph)
    raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw) & (raw > 1e-4), raw, 1e-4)
    return _normalize_internal(raw, n)


def _tile_start(n):
    base = np.array([0.2, 0.4, 0.2, 0.2], dtype=np.float64)
    return _upscale(base, n, mode="repeat")


def _lp_response(q, n, rng, time_limit):
    if linprog is None or n > 2048:
        return None
    try:
        b = _conv_square(q)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            return None
        L = 2 * n - 1
        scale = float(n)
        max_rows = 2000
        if L <= max_rows:
            rows = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
        else:
            rows = np.argsort(b, kind="stable")[::-1][:max_rows].astype(int)

        A = np.zeros((rows.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
        qs = q * scale
        for r, kk in enumerate(rows):
            s = kk - (n - 1)
            if s < 0:
                s = 0
            e = kk
            if e >= n:
                e = n - 1
            if s <= e:
                A[r, s:e + 1] = qs[kk - e:kk - s + 1][::-1]

        b_ub = np.full(rows.size, M * scale, dtype=np.float64)
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        c += 1e-5 * (q - float(np.mean(q)))
        c += 1e-6 * rng.normal(size=n)

        res = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=(0.0, 100.0),
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(0.5, time_limit))},
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=(0.0, 100.0),
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True},
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

        x = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
        if x is None:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return None
        g = g / s

        try:
            viol = float(np.max(A @ g - b_ub))
        except Exception:
            viol = 0.0
        tol = 1e-4 * max(1.0, float(np.max(b_ub)))
        if viol > tol:
            return None
        return g
    except Exception:
        return None


def _gradient_polish(q, end_time, rng, consider_func=None):
    n = q.size
    q = _project_simplex(q, n)
    best = q.copy()
    best_s = _score_norm(best)
    if consider_func is not None:
        consider_func(best.copy())

    bmax0 = best_s / (2.0 * max(1, n))
    tau = max(1e-12, 0.25 * bmax0 if bmax0 > 0 else 1e-12)
    no_imp = 0
    radius = 1.0 / float(np.sqrt(max(1, n)))
    lower = (2.0 * n) / max(1, (2 * n - 1))

    for it in range(1_000_000):
        try:
            if time.monotonic() > end_time:
                break

            grad, bmax = _smoothed_grad(best, tau)
            if grad.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                break

            d = -(grad - float(np.mean(grad)))
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))

            local_best = best.copy()
            local_s = best_s

            if dn > 1e-14:
                unit = d / dn
                for mult in (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0):
                    if time.monotonic() > end_time:
                        break
                    cand = best + (mult * radius) * unit
                    cand = _project_simplex(cand, n)
                    sc = _score_norm(cand)
                    if sc < local_s - 1e-12:
                        local_s = sc
                        local_best = cand.copy()

            g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
            maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if maxg > 1e-14:
                for mult in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0):
                    if time.monotonic() > end_time:
                        break
                    logw = -(mult / maxg) * g
                    logw = np.minimum(np.maximum(logw, -50.0), 50.0)
                    cand = best * np.exp(logw)
                    cand = _project_simplex(cand, n)
                    sc = _score_norm(cand)
                    if sc < local_s - 1e-12:
                        local_s = sc
                        local_best = cand.copy()

            if it % 20 == 19 or local_s >= best_s - 1e-13:
                for _ in range(2):
                    if time.monotonic() > end_time:
                        break
                    v = rng.normal(size=n)
                    v -= float(v.mean())
                    vn = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
                    if vn <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    v /= vn
                    for mult in (0.03, 0.1, 0.3):
                        cand = best + (mult * radius) * v
                        cand = _project_simplex(cand, n)
                        sc = _score_norm(cand)
                        if sc < local_s - 1e-12:
                            local_s = sc
                            local_best = cand.copy()

            if local_s < best_s - 1e-12:
                old_s = best_s
                best = local_best.copy()
                best_s = local_s
                no_imp = 0
                if consider_func is not None and (old_s - best_s > 5e-7 or it % 25 == 0):
                    consider_func(best.copy())
            else:
                no_imp += 1
                tau = min(tau * 1.25, max(1e-10, 0.5 * bmax0))
                if no_imp >= 35:
                    kicked = False
                    v = rng.normal(size=n)
                    v -= float(v.mean())
                    vn = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
                    if vn > 0.0:
                        v /= vn
                        for mult in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0):
                            cand = best + (mult * radius) * v
                            cand = _project_simplex(cand, n)
                            sc = _score_norm(cand)
                            if sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                                best = cand.copy()
                                best_s = sc
                                no_imp = 0
                                kicked = True
                                if consider_func is not None:
                                    consider_func(best.copy())
                                break
                    if not kicked:
                        break

            tau *= 0.99
            tau = max(tau, 1e-12)
            if best_s <= lower + 1e-9:
                break
        except Exception:
            break

    if consider_func is not None:
        consider_func(best.copy())
    return best, best_s


def _optimize(q0, n, rng, end_time, lp_iters=12, consider_func=None):
    try:
        q = _project_simplex(np.asarray(q0, dtype=np.float64), n)
        best = q.copy()
        best_s = _score_norm(best)
        if consider_func is not None:
            consider_func(best.copy())

        now = time.monotonic()
        if (
            linprog is not None
            and lp_iters > 0
            and n <= 1024
            and (end_time - now) > 12.0
        ):
            lp_end = min(end_time, now + max(8.0, 0.25 * max(0.0, end_time - now)))
            stagnation = 0
            for it in range(max(1, int(lp_iters))):
                if time.monotonic() > lp_end - 6.0:
                    break
                remaining = max(1, int(lp_iters) - it)
                tlim = min(30.0, max(1.0, (lp_end - time.monotonic() - 2.0) / float(remaining)))
                if tlim < 0.25:
                    break
                g = _lp_response(q, n, rng, tlim)
                if g is None:
                    break

                local_best = best.copy()
                local_s = best_s
                for t in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
                    cand = (1.0 - t) * best + t * g
                    cand = _project_simplex(cand, n)
                    sc = _score_norm(cand)
                    if sc < local_s - 1e-12:
                        local_s = sc
                        local_best = cand.copy()

                if local_s < best_s - 1e-12:
                    old_s = best_s
                    best = local_best.copy()
                    best_s = local_s
                    q = best.copy()
                    stagnation = 0
                    if consider_func is not None and (old_s - best_s > 5e-7 or it % 3 == 0):
                        consider_func(best.copy())
                else:
                    stagnation += 1
                    if stagnation >= 4:
                        break

        if time.monotonic() < end_time - 2.0:
            q, best_s = _gradient_polish(q, end_time, rng, consider_func)
            best = q.copy()

        if consider_func is not None:
            consider_func(best.copy())
        return best, best_s
    except Exception:
        try:
            q = _project_simplex(np.asarray(q0, dtype=np.float64), n)
            if consider_func is not None:
                consider_func(q.copy())
            return q, _score_norm(q)
        except Exception:
            uni = np.full(max(1, n), 1.0 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
            if consider_func is not None:
                consider_func(uni.copy())
            return uni, _score_norm(uni)


def _dedupe_starts(starts):
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for a in starts:
        try:
            arr = _normalize_internal(a, 512)
            if arr.size == 0:
                continue
            key = np.round(arr, 10).tobytes()
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            out.append(arr)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return out


def _lp_iters_for(n, time_left):
    if n <= 64:
        return 30
    if n <= 128:
        return 20
    if n <= 256:
        return 14
    if n <= 512:
        return 12
    if n <= 1024:
        return 6
    return 0


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    headroom = 30.0 if budget_s > 90.0 else max(2.0, 0.1 * budget_s)
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, budget_s - headroom)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            parent = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parent.size == 0:
                parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    main_n = 512
    if parent is not None and 16 <= parent.size <= 2048:
        main_n = int(parent.size)
    main_n = int(max(16, min(main_n, 1024)))

    best_arr = None
    best_list = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    def consider(a):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_list, best_exact
        try:
            c = _clean_for_eval(a, main_n)
            if c.size == 0:
                c = np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = _safe_exact(c)
            if np.isfinite(v) and v < best_exact - 1e-15:
                best_exact = float(v)
                best_arr = c.copy()
                best_list = [float(x) for x in c]
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Baselines.
    try:
        consider(np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64))
        if parent is not None:
            consider(parent)
        consider(_tile_start(main_n))
        consider(_bell_start(main_n, center=True, rng=rng))
        consider(_bell_start(main_n, center=False, rng=rng))
        consider(_cosine_start(main_n, rng))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # If the budget is too small, preserve the best valid baseline.
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
        if best_arr is None:
            best_arr = np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64)
            best_list = [float(x) for x in best_arr]
        return [float(x) for x in best_list] if best_list is not None else [1.0]

    # Coarse-to-fine warm-up.
    prev_small = None
    if parent is None:
        parent = np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64)

    coarse_time = min(420.0, 0.27 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if coarse_time > 40.0:
        coarse_specs = [
            (16, 0.5),
            (32, 1.0),
            (64, 2.0),
            (128, 4.0),
            (256, 8.0),
        ]
        coarse_specs = [(k, w) for k, w in coarse_specs if k < main_n]
        sw = float(sum(w for _, w in coarse_specs)) if coarse_specs else 1.0

        for kk, ww in coarse_specs:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 35.0:
                break
            alloc = coarse_time * (ww / sw)
            if alloc < 5.0:
                continue
            end = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
            starts = []
            starts.append(_upscale(parent, kk, "interp"))
            if prev_small is not None:
                starts.append(_upscale(prev_small, kk, "interp"))
                starts.append(_upscale(prev_small, kk, "repeat"))
            starts.append(np.full(kk, 1.0 / kk, dtype=np.float64))
            starts.append(_bell_start(kk, center=True, rng=rng))
            starts.append(_bell_start(kk, center=False, rng=rng))
            starts = _dedupe_starts(starts)[:4]

            local_best = None
            local_s = float("inf")
            fracs = (0.55, 0.30, 0.15)
            for i, q0 in enumerate(starts):
                if time.monotonic() > end - 2.0:
                    break
                frac = fracs[i] if i < len(fracs) else 0.05
                sub_end = min(end, time.monotonic() + abs(alloc) * frac)
                if sub_end - time.monotonic() < 2.0:
                    continue
                qopt, s = _optimize(
                    q0,
                    kk,
                    rng,
                    sub_end,
                    lp_iters=_lp_iters_for(kk, max(0.0, sub_end - time.monotonic())),
                    consider_func=consider,
                )
                consider(qopt)
                if s < local_s:
                    local_s = s
                    local_best = qopt

            if local_best is not None:
                prev_small = local_best

    # Main length stage.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 50.0:
        main_starts = []
        try:
            main_starts.append(_upscale(parent, main_n, "interp"))
            if prev_small is not None:
                main_starts.append(_upscale(prev_small, main_n, "interp"))
                main_starts.append(_upscale(prev_small, main_n, "repeat"))
            main_starts.append(_tile_start(main_n))
            main_starts.append(_bell_start(main_n, center=True, rng=rng))
            main_starts.append(_bell_start(main_n, center=False, rng=rng))
            main_starts.append(_cosine_start(main_n, rng))
            main_starts.append(np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64))
            main_starts = _dedupe_starts(main_starts)
        except Exception:
            main_starts = [np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64)]

        scored = []
        for q in main_starts[:8]:
            try:
                sc = _evaluate_clean(q, main_n)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    sc = _score_norm(q)
                scored.append((float(sc), q))
            except Exception:
                scored.append((float("inf"), q))
        if scored:
            scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

        if len(scored) == 1:
            allocated = [1.0]
        elif len(scored) == 2:
            allocated = [0.6, 0.4]
        elif len(scored) == 3:
            allocated = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]
        elif len(scored) == 4:
            allocated = [0.45, 0.25, 0.2, 0.1]
        else:
            allocated = [0.42, 0.24, 0.16, 0.10, 0.08]

        main_budget = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        for i, (_, q0) in enumerate(scored[:5]):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                break
            frac = allocated[i] if i < len(allocated) else 0.05
            alloc = main_budget * frac
            if i > 0 and alloc < 12.0:
                break
            end = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
            _optimize(
                q0,
                main_n,
                rng,
                end,
                lp_iters=_lp_iters_for(main_n, max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())),
                consider_func=consider,
            )

    # Optional larger-length continuation if time remains.
    if (
        best_arr is not None
        and main_n <= 768
        and time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0
    ):
        n_big = 1024
        try:
            q_big = _upscale(best_arr, n_big, "interp")
            end_big = min(deadline - 12.0, time.monotonic() + max(120.0, 0.35 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
            _optimize(
                q_big,
                n_big,
                rng,
                end_big,
                lp_iters=4,
                consider_func=consider,
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Very short final local search on the exact best.
    try:
        if best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
            q = best_arr.copy()
            s = _score_norm(q)
            nq = q.size
            rad = 1.0 / float(np.sqrt(max(1, nq)))
            for _ in range(200):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 4.0:
                    break
                v = rng.normal(size=nq)
                v -= float(v.mean())
                vn = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
                if vn <= 0.0:
                    continue
                v /= vn
                for mult in (0.005, 0.02, 0.08, 0.2):
                    cand = q + (mult * rad) * v
                    cand = _project_simplex(cand, nq)
                    sc = _score_norm(cand)
                    if sc < s - 1e-10:
                        q = cand.copy()
                        s = sc
                        consider(q)
        else:
            if best_arr is None:
                best_arr = np.full(main_n, 1.0 / main_n, dtype=np.float64)
                best_list = [float(x) for x in best_arr]
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Final validation.
    if best_list is None or len(best_list) == 0:
        best_arr = np.full(max(16, main_n), 1.0 / max(16, main_n), dtype=np.float64)
        best_list = [float(x) for x in best_arr]

    try:
        vv = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
        if not np.isfinite(vv):
            raise ValueError("invalid final score")
    except Exception:
        fb = np.full(max(16, main_n), 1.0 / max(16, main_n), dtype=np.float64)
        best_list = [float(x) for x in fb]

    return [float(x) for x in best_list]
```
